<div class="card-body w-100 cp-card__container">
<h2 class="card-title cp-card__header">CloseCAPTURE</h2>
<p class="cp-card__content">Reduce loan fallout. Optimize ROI. Capture more closings. With this flexible program lenders can reduce credit report costs, help applicants whose scores are close to qualifying, obtain quality leads, identify where lost sales went and why, and retain their portfolios.</p>

<p class="cp-card__content">A comprehensive lead generation program. We even offer a turnkey solution whereby you can send a firm offer of credit to these leads.</p>

        Class Description
      </a>
<div class="collapse" id="collapseDescription-314">
<p class="card-description__description">
<span style="font-weight: 400;">You’ll learn how to better protect against mortgage fraud by using Tax Return Verification Reports. </span> </p>
